- **cline**: 把编码任务外包给 Agent Hub 上的 cline worker（cline CLI `--json` NDJSON 无头模式，模型参数 -m，超时 --timeout，工作目录 --cwd，附件以 @路径 写进提示词）。何时用：用户点名「cline」；或需要与其他 worker 并行分摊编码任务。何时不用：用户点名其他代理时；空跑任务。你只做派发与结果回收，不自己实现代码。 ([Johhny-Xu/multi-agent-hub](https://github.com/Johhny-Xu/multi-agent-hub/blob/HEAD/plugins/agenthub-workers/agents/cline.md) · ★14)
- **cline**: Cline solves these challenges by operating as an **Autonomous Software Engineer inside the IDE**. It proactively explores codebase hierarchies, executes terminal commands, inspects browser rendering output, diagnoses test failures, and iteratively applies multi-file fixes until tasks pass all verifications. ([joanmarcriera/Home-office-automations](https://github.com/joanmarcriera/Home-office-automations/blob/HEAD/docs/tools/agents/cline.md) · ★7)
- **cline**: 核实基线：源码（Cline 开源仓库 apps/vscode 的 openDiskConversationHistory.ts/disk.ts）+ 本机实测（2026-09-03，VS Code 1.103.2 + Cline 4.1.17 一条真实对话）；读取器 `agentsync.readers.read_cline`（第 15 家读取源）。 ([Chendestiny/agent-session-sync](https://github.com/Chendestiny/agent-session-sync/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/cline.md) · ★5 · 2 copies)
- **cline**: Cline reads skills from `.cline/skills/` (project) and `~/.cline/skills/` (user) and does not document `.agents/skills/`. It has a built-in subagent tool but no custom subagent files. ([requie/endostack](https://github.com/requie/endostack/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/cline.md) · ★4)
- **cline**: &gt; Setup for SIGIL with Cline — Moderate coverage (Layers 2-4 active, limited hooks via VS Code). ([jedarden/SIGIL](https://github.com/jedarden/SIGIL/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/cline.md) · ★3)
- **cline**: Cline's standalone terminal agent (`cline`) — the CLI sibling of the Cline IDE extension — run autonomously under Bivy (`cline -y`, which skips per-tool prompts so a piped run doesn't wedge on approval; Bivy's sandbox tier still bounds what it can actually do). ([bivysh/bivy](https://github.com/bivysh/bivy/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/cline.md) · ★2)
- **cline**: &gt; Generated from `api/agent_registry.py` — do not edit by hand. &gt; Refresh with `python scripts/generate_agent_docs.py`. ([Hotsteel2901/Auto-Get-PY](https://github.com/Hotsteel2901/Auto-Get-PY/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/cline.md) · ★2)
- **cline**: Stonewall runs Cline CLI inside a kernel-enforced sandbox. The agent only sees your project directory and the few binaries and paths the policy names. Everything else on your machine, from `~/.ssh` to your browser profile, is invisible to it, no matter what a prompt says. ([stonewall-sh/stonewall](https://github.com/stonewall-sh/stonewall/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/cline.md) · ★2)
- **cline**: **Checked:** 2026-10-04. **Confidence:** medium; official documentation describes task semantics and local history but not a stable third-party export contract. ([the-toji-zenin/spider-web](https://github.com/the-toji-zenin/spider-web/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/cline.md) · ★2)
- **cline**: Each person on the team runs this on the machine where Cline is installed. Cline then joins the same room as everyone else's agents. ([Uuriko/project-room](https://github.com/Uuriko/project-room/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/cline.md) · ★2)
- **cline**: **Project:** Cline (autonomous AI coding VS Code extension) **Language:** TypeScript **Repository:** cline/cline ([qiangli/ycode](https://github.com/qiangli/ycode/blob/HEAD/docs/research/agents/cline.md) · ★1)
- **cline**: - If `.clinerules` is a **directory** (current Cline) or missing, writes `.clinerules/localcloud.md` (no frontmatter = always active). - If `.clinerules` is a single **file** (older setups), the localcloud block is appended to it. - If only `.cline/rules/` exists, the rule goes there. ([rayhankhilji/localcloud](https://github.com/rayhankhilji/localcloud/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/cline.md) · ★1)
- **cline**: **English.** In Plan mode, attach the generated brief to the task and inspect the target workspace rules before switching to Act. Executes a single clou unit-graph unit given a pre-assembled prompt body containing Goal / Inputs (resolved) / Output / Contract / Verification; produces the output file specified; stops when the contract is met. ([ejmockler/clou](https://github.com/ejmockler/clou/blob/HEAD/agents/clou-executor.md))
- **clou-supervisor**: clou's supervisor — graph-driven, contract-enforced unit-execution discipline. Advises and acts on units in the project's `.clou/graph/` unit-graph. Use when the user wants disciplined work on a specific unit (or set of units), or asks to "proceed continuously" through a deliverable's units. Invoke via the `/clou-execute U###` slash command or directly via `Agent(subagent_type="clou-ai:clou-supervisor", ...)`. ([ejmockler/clou](https://github.com/ejmockler/clou/blob/HEAD/agents/clou-supervisor.md))

- **cloud-functions-specialist.ci-wiring.knowledge**: - **Prove endpoint config, never reason about it:** `npm run test:deploy-manifest` imports the ENTRY POINT (the only way the global call runs), pinning region, numeric `maxInstances` and the cascades' `concurrency === 1`. An unset v2 option is a sentinel OBJECT, not null (`== null` is FALSE) — check `typeof x === "number"`. Vacuity is in the `gcfv2` FILTER: guard the filtered COUNT per caller, kee ([Malingisslen/butlery](https://github.com/Malingisslen/butlery/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cloud-functions-specialist.ci-wiring.knowledge.md) · ★1)
- **cloud-functions-specialist.client-guards.knowledge**: - **Client burst guards are `rateLimitStamped(type, s, key)`** (ADR-0020): same-request stamp on `request.time` + `lastDocId`. A COMPOSITE key joins ids with `/` (no id holds it): `'_'` lets `a_b`+`c` and `a`+`b_c` share one stamp.
